## 任务：收集 Qwen-7B s0 ARC eval 完整结果

### 背景
Qwen-7B s0 ARC 25-shot eval 在 cqa1-21977 运行，d000=87.20% 已完成，d005-d100 应接近或已完成。Cron 反复误报（eval 不用 W&B）。

### 步骤
SSH 到 cqa1-21977（ssh_read_only）：
```bash
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base
```

1. **检查进程**：
```bash
ps aux | grep -i "eval\|evaluate" | grep -v grep
nvidia-smi
```

2. **检查所有 ARC 结果**：
```bash
for d in d000 d005 d025 d050 d100; do
    echo "=== ARC $d ==="
    find /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results_v2/qwen7b_${d}_s0/ -name "*arc*" -exec cat {} \; 2>/dev/null
    ls /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results_v2/qwen7b_${d}_s0/*arc*.done 2>/dev/null && echo "DONE" || echo "NOT DONE"
done
```

3. **如果全部完成**：
   计算 Δ(d100−d000) 并更新 registry：
   - exp_id: eval_qwen7b_mmlu_arc_s0
   - status: DONE, conclusion: success
   - metrics 包含全部 MMLU + ARC 数据：
     MMLU: d000=66.32%, d005=64.68%, d025=65.67%, d050=65.78%, d100=65.29%, Δ=-1.03pp
     ARC: d000=87.20%, d005=?, d025=?, d050=?, d100=?, Δ=?

4. **如果仍在跑**：等 3 分钟再检查一次。

Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
